  drop

route-policy PASS
  pass
end-policy
!
route-policy LABEL
  if destination in LABEL then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy SID08
  set community (1204:08)
end-policy
!
route-policy U2000
  if destination in U2000 then
    set tag 2
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy qosip
  if destination in (192.168.30.0/30) then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy RR-NHS
  if path-type is ebgp then
    set next-hop self
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy DEFAULT
  if destination in default then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy set-wlp
  set weight 0
  set local-preference 0
end-policy
!
route-policy qosiprpl
  if destination in qosip then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy

end-policy
